; The koalas in the pixel look (DESIGN §3, D30), generated from the smooth
; art, with the faces stamped on from the hand-drawn sitting koala: an Ink
; outline, Fog fur shaded Lilac and lit Mist. Each sprite is as wide
; either side of the koala's feet, so it mirrors about them. Slopes are in
; steps of a 32nd of a turn, the somersault (tumble) in 16 steps; walking
; has 6 frames a stride.
colours i=Ink d=Dusk l=Lilac f=Fog m=Mist w=White s=Sage F=Fern
